- **es-hcm-calc-adjusted-calcium**: Computes albumin‑adjusted (corrected) serum calcium using total calcium and albumin to adjust for hypo‑ or hyperalbuminemia. - **es-hcm-calcitriol-high-add-bp-dmab**: Recommends adding intravenous bisphosphonate or denosumab in adults with hypercalcemia of malignancy due to tumors with high calcitriol levels (e.g., lymphoma) who remain severe or symptomatic despite glucocorticoid therapy. - **esa-pa-case-detection-screening**: We need to produce a SKILL.md file with the format: ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-case-detection-screening) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-decide-avs-use**: Determines whether to perform adrenal venous sampling (AVS) to lateralize aldosterone excess in patients with confirmed primary aldosteronism (PA) who are being evaluated for surgical treatment. Indicated when surgery is feasible and desired by the patient, or when subtype workup is planned for a surgical candidate, especially in those younger than 35 years with spontaneous hypokalemia, marked aldosterone excess, and unilateral adrenal lesions on CT. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-decide-avs-use) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-gra-add-mra**: Determines whether to add a mineralocorticoid receptor antagonist (e.g., spironolactone or eplerenone) to glucocorticoid therapy in glucocorticoid-remediable aldosteronism when blood pressure fails to normalize with glucocorticoid alone. Triggered by persistent hypertension or inadequate BP control despite optimized glucocorticoid dosing in GRA patients. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-gra-add-mra) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-gra-gluco-dosing**: Calculates the lowest effective glucocorticoid dose (dexamethasone or prednisone) to normalize blood pressure and serum potassium in glucocorticoid-remediable aldosteronism (GRA/FH-I) by titrating to biochemical and clinical targets. Indicated when initiating medical treatment for confirmed GRA, triggered by findings such as early-onset hypertension, family history of stroke before age 40, spontaneous hypokalemia, or suppressed plasma renin activity with elevated aldosterone. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-gra-gluco-dosing) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-18ohb**: Differentiates aldosterone-producing adenoma from idiopathic adrenal hyperplasia based on 18-hydroxycorticosterone levels, with APA patients generally having levels &gt;100 ng/dL at 8:00 a.m. and IAH patients usually having levels &lt;100 ng/dL. Use when reviewing 18-OHB test results to help subtype PA; triggers include 18-OHB &gt;100 ng/dL (suggesting APA) or &lt;100 ng/dL (suggesting IAH). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-18ohb) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-18oxo**: Helps distinguish unilateral from bilateral adrenal disease based on 18-oxocortisol levels, which are typically higher in aldosterone-producing adenoma than idiopathic adrenal hyperplasia. Use when evaluating 18-oxocortisol results during PA workup to guide subtype classification after a positive ARR. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-18oxo) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-avs**: Determines unilateral vs bilateral aldosterone excess based on cortisol-corrected aldosterone ratios from adrenal venous sampling, with ratio &gt;4:1 indicating unilateral excess, ratio 3:1 suggestive of bilateral hypersecretion, and interpretation dependent on cosyntropin stimulation protocol used during sampling. Use when analyzing AVS results to guide surgical vs medical treatment decisions; triggers include 'AVS lateralization ratio', 'cosyntropin-stimulated AVS', 'aldosterone-to-cortisol ratio &gt;4:1', and 'ratio 3:1 suggestive of bilateral disease'. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-avs) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-cct**: Evaluates likelihood of primary aldosteronism by measuring plasma aldosterone suppression after oral captopril; normal suppression ≥30% makes PA unlikely, while lack of suppression with persistently suppressed plasma renin activity suggests PA. Use when assessing captopril challenge test (CCT) results for PA diagnosis in patients with positive aldosterone-to-renin ratio. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-cct) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-fst**: Determines if primary aldosteronism is confirmed based on upright plasma aldosterone ≤6 ng/dL (170 nmol/L) on day 4 at 10 AM of fludrocortisone suppression test, provided plasma renin activity ≥1 ng/mL/h and plasma cortisol concentration lower than the 7 AM value to exclude ACTH confounding. Use when reviewing FST results to diagnose PA; triggers include "FST", "upright aldosterone ≤6 ng/dL", "PRA ≥1 ng/mL/h", and "cortisol drop". ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-fst) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-oral-sodium-loading**: This skill interprets the oral sodium loading test for primary aldosteronism by evaluating 24‑hour urinary aldosterone excretion after a high‑sodium diet. Use when reviewing results of an "oral sodium loading test" for PA diagnosis; trigger phrases include "urinary aldosterone excretion ≤10 g/24 h" (PA unlikely) or "≥12 g/24 h (Mayo) / ≥14 g/24 h (Cleveland)" (PA highly likely). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-oral-sodium-loading) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-postural-stim**: Interprets the postural stimulation test to detect angiotensin II‑unresponsive aldosterone‑producing adenoma (APA) or familial hyperaldosteronism type I (FH‑I) when aldosterone fails to rise with upright posture. Triggered by clinical cues such as “postural stimulation test shows no aldosterone increase”, “suspected APA with non‑responsive aldosterone”, or “evaluating FH‑I in young patients with early‑onset hypertension and hypokalemia”. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-interpret-postural-stim) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-interpret-sit**: Interprets the saline infusion test (SIT) to assess the probability of primary aldosteronism when a patient has a positive aldosterone-to-renin ratio (ARR) and requires confirmatory testing. Triggers include evaluating post‑infusion plasma aldosterone concentration (PAC) after a positive ARR, hypertension work‑up, or when deciding whether to proceed to adrenal venous sampling (AVS). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/esa-pa-pediatric-bp-target) · ★12)
- **esa-pa-pediatric-gluco-dose**: Determines age- and body weight-appropriate glucocorticoid dosage for pediatric patients with primary aldosteronism, particularly glucocorticoid-remediable aldosteronism (GRA), to avoid over-treatment. Also covers the ESMC-{300M,600M,6B} protein language models from the same release: masked-LM logits, hidden states, mutation scoring, contact prediction, and the SAE interpretability head. Triggered when evaluating overweight/obese youth (BMI ≥85th percentile) and considering liver enzyme testing. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-alt-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-bariatric-surgery-contraindication**: Assesses contraindications for bariatric surgery in adolescents, recommending against the procedure in pre‑adolescent children, pregnant or breast‑feeding teens, and those who have not mastered healthy dietary and activity habits or have unresolved substance use, eating disorder, or untreated psychiatric disorder. Clinical triggers include Tanner stage &lt;4, positive pregnancy test, lactation, ongoing substance abuse, active eating disorder, or untreated psychiatric comorbidity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-bariatric-surgery-contraindication) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-bariatric-surgery-eligibility**: Assesses eligibility for bariatric surgery in adolescents with obesity. Trigger phrases include Tanner 4-5 pubertal development, BMI ≥40 kg/m2 or BMI ≥35 kg/m2 with significant comorbidities, failed lifestyle modification, and need for psychological stability. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-bariatric-surgery-eligibility) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-bmi-classification**: Determines if a child ≥2 years is overweight, obese, or extremely obese using BMI percentile and extreme obesity thresholds. Triggers include BMI ≥85th percentile (overweight), ≥95th percentile (obese), or ≥120% of the 95th percentile or ≥35 kg/m2 (extremely obese). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-bmi-classification) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-bmi-monitoring**: The skill calculates, plots, and reviews a child's BMI percentile at least once per year during well-child or sick-child visits. It triggers when a pediatric patient presents for routine health supervision or acute care, prompting BMI assessment and longitudinal tracking. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-bmi-monitoring) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-bp-interpretation**: This skill interprets blood pressure using age-, sex-, and height-specific percentile norms to screen for prehypertension and hypertension in pediatric patients. Trigger phrases include BP ≥90th to &lt;95th percentile = prehypertension and BP ≥95th percentile = stage 1 hypertension (with age‑specific adjustments). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-bp-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-comorbidity-trigger**: This skill recommends evaluating children and adolescents with a BMI at or above the 85th percentile for age and sex for potential obesity-related comorbidities. Clinical triggers include overweight or obese classification by BMI percentile. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-comorbidity-trigger) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-endocrine-lab-trigger**: Recommends against routine laboratory evaluation for endocrine etiologies of pediatric obesity unless the patient exhibits attenuated stature or decreased height velocity relative to genetic/familial potential and pubertal stage. Clinical trigger phrases include short stature for target height, slowed growth velocity, or crossing downward on height percentile curves. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-endocrine-lab-trigger) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-fasting-glucose-interpretation**: Interprets fasting plasma glucose to screen for prediabetes and diabetes in children and adolescents with BMI ≥85th percentile (overweight/obese). Trigger phrases include 'overweight or obese', 'BMI ≥85th percentile', 'screening for comorbidities', and 'fasting plasma glucose'. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-fasting-glucose-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-genetic-test-trigger**: This skill suggests genetic testing for pediatric patients with extreme early onset obesity (onset before 5 years of age) who exhibit clinical features of genetic obesity syndromes such as extreme hyperphagia, or who have a family history of extreme obesity. It helps clinicians identify when to refer for genetic evaluation to diagnose rare monogenic obesity syndromes. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-genetic-test-trigger) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-hba1c-interpretation**: Interprets HbA1c to screen for prediabetes and diabetes in overweight or obese children (BMI ≥85th percentile) being evaluated for comorbidities. Triggers include HbA1c results, BMI percentile, and clinical suspicion of dysglycemia. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-hba1c-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-hdl-interpretation**: Interprets HDL cholesterol levels to screen for dyslipidemia in pediatric patients using thresholds of &lt;40 mg/dL low, 40–45 mg/dL borderline low, and ≥45 mg/dL acceptable. Triggered during lipid panel review for overweight or obese children (BMI ≥85th percentile) or when evaluating for cardiometabolic risk. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-hdl-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-healthy-eating**: Prescribes specific healthy eating habits for pediatric obesity prevention and treatment, focusing on avoiding calorie-dense nutrient-poor foods and encouraging whole fruits over juice. Trigger phrases include well-child visit, BMI ≥85th percentile, excessive juice intake, frequent fast food or sugary beverage consumption. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-healthy-eating) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-infant-diagnosis**: Diagnoses obesity in infants and toddlers under 2 years of age when sex-specific weight-for-recumbent length is at or above the 97.7th percentile on WHO growth charts. Consider this assessment during well-child visits when caregivers report excessive weight gain, crossing of percentile channels, or when length and weight measurements suggest disproportionate adiposity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-infant-diagnosis) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-ldl-interpretation**: Interprets LDL cholesterol levels to screen for dyslipidemia in children and adolescents with obesity (BMI ≥85th percentile). Acceptable &lt;110 mg/dL, borderline high 110-129 mg/dL, high ≥130 mg/dL triggers further evaluation or lifestyle intervention. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-ldl-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-non-hdl-interpretation**: We need to output a SKILL.md file with the specified format. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-non-hdl-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-ogtt-glucose-interpretation**: Interprets the 2-hour plasma glucose from an oral glucose tolerance test (OGTT) to screen for prediabetes and diabetes in children and adolescents with overweight or obesity. Uses thresholds of &lt;140 mg/dL (normal), 140–199 mg/dL (impaired glucose tolerance, IGT), and ≥200 mg/dL (diabetes) per ADA criteria referenced in the guideline. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-ogtt-glucose-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-pcos-hormone-interpretation**: Interprets free and total testosterone and SHBG levels to screen for hyperandrogenism in adolescents with obesity, using Endocrine Society PCOS guidelines. Trigger phrases include oligomenorrhea, hirsutism, acne, and elevated androgen symptoms prompting PCOS evaluation. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-pcos-hormone-interpretation) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-admin**: Determines when to administer FDA-approved obesity pharmacotherapy in pediatric patients, requiring a concomitant lifestyle modification program of the highest intensity and prescribing only by experienced clinicians. Triggers include documented failure of intensive lifestyle modification to limit weight gain or ameliorate comorbidities in patients with obesity (BMI ≥95th percentile). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-admin) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-discontinue**: Determines when to discontinue obesity pharmacotherapy due to lack of efficacy. Trigger phrases include “&lt;4% BMI/BMI z score reduction after 12 weeks at full dosage” or “insufficient weight loss on antiobesity medication.” ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-discontinue) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-initiate**: Determines when to initiate pharmacotherapy for pediatric obesity after a formal intensive lifestyle modification program fails to limit weight gain or ameliorate comorbidities. Trigger phrases include "failed intensive lifestyle modification," "no weight loss despite lifestyle program," and "persistent comorbidities despite lifestyle intervention." ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-initiate) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-physical-activity**: Prescribes physical activity for pediatric obesity prevention and treatment in children and adolescents with BMI at or above the 85th percentile. Recommends at least 20 minutes, optimally 60 minutes, of vigorous physical activity on ≥5 days per week to improve metabolic health and reduce obesity risk. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-physical-activity) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-psychosocial-eval**: This skill guides clinicians to evaluate for psychosocial comorbidities in pediatric obesity and to initiate assessment/counseling when psychosocial problems are suspected. Trigger phrases include family dysfunction, stressors, bullying, low self-esteem, depression, anxiety, eating disorders, or substance abuse. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-psychosocial-eval) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-rearing-patterns**: This skill guides clinicians to identify maladaptive rearing patterns related to diet, activity, and screen time in pediatric patients with overweight or obesity. It prompts evaluation of screen time, dietary habits, physical activity, and family eating cues to target education on healthy food and exercise habits. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-rearing-patterns) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-screen-time**: Recommends limiting non-academic screen time to 1-2 hours per day and decreasing other sedentary behaviors. Trigger phrases include "excessive screen time," "digital activities," and "sedentary behaviors." ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-screen-time) · ★12)
- **eso-pediatric-obesity-total-cholesterol-interpretation**: This skill interprets fasting total cholesterol levels to screen for dyslipidemia in children and adolescents undergoing obesity evaluation. Triggered by pediatric obesity screening (BMI ≥85th percentile) or dyslipidemia evaluation, it classifies cholesterol as acceptable (&lt;170 mg/dL), borderline high (170‑199 mg/dL), or high (≥200 mg/dL) per Endocrine Society guidelines. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-total-cholesterol-interpretation) · ★12)

- **eso-pediatric-obesity-triglycerides-10-19-years**: Interprets fasting triglyceride levels to screen for dyslipidemia in overweight or obese children aged 10-19 years. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/eso-pediatric-obesity-triglycerides-10-19-years) · ★12)
